## Rules
- Pool (1.4 rule, docs/evidence/compiler_pin.md:8-17): proto_map class identical; one targetVA; unit (units.toml `text_start <= va < text_end`) kind game and delink true; not tail (protoVA - 0x400400 in 0x13BD4E..0x13F2F4); VA not a retail_folds retailVA; a symbols.txt `func` line at the VA.
- Rank: (size, VA) ascending. A row passes iff every check passes.
- sig: decorated symbols.txt name (`?` prefix), or the PDB S_*PROC32* at the protoVA with a prototype name has a type index whose TPI leaf is LF_PROCEDURE/LF_MFUNCTION (0x0008/0x0009 16t or 0x1008/0x1009); fails also when the row has no prototype names.
- reloc: sites = proto_map.mask_proc on the retail bytes (abs all; rel only if the target is outside [va, va+size)). Resolved iff target inside own extent, == a symbols table func VA, or inside a symbols table data row [va, va+size) (size `-` = exact VA, or the member extent [va, min(va + S, next pinned VA)) when the PDB public of that name has S_GDATA32/S_LDATA32 records of one type size S; the site then carries `member` name+offset), with the row status in units_seed.STATUSES (unverified, verified) or the pin status (disasm-verified, corr-verified), or near-corr on a func row only (D115; 4.2 T9). Tables: config/retail/symbols.txt, then config/retail/symbols_pin.txt (as gate.py). "provisional" = a resolving row's status outside both.
- c0035: rel sites whose target lies in the unit's [text_start, text_end) and outside own extent: each must carry a reloc in the unit's cut object .text at offset va + site - text_start (object sha256 == its hashes.txt line; function symbol offsets == va - text_start).
- bodymax: a Ghidra applied functions.jsonl entry == VA and va+size-1 <= bodyMax, or every byte of (bodyMax, va+size) lies inside the 4-byte field of a reloc in the unit's cut object .text (object offset = va - text_start; a jump table; same object checks as c0035).
- library: fails on a `std::` prototype name, `@std@@` in the decorated name, or an XDK module path (`obj\i386\`, `D:\xboxfre`, `* Linker *`).

## Detectors (iced-x86 32-bit decode of [va, va+size))
- float: an instruction whose first non-prefix byte is an x87 escape D8..DF.
- switch: `jmp [index*4+disp32]` with no base register.
- bittest: bt/bts/btr/btc, or `test` with a power-of-two immediate.
- inlined: always `?` (no reliable detector: short inline procs are mostly proto_map ambiguous, C0010; the 1.4 containment scan gave 0 pool hits).
- vcall: `call` with a memory operand that has a base register.
- eh (compiler_pin.md:21-22): an `fs:[0]` access; or a near call to a proc whose first 4 instructions touch `fs:[0]`; or `push imm32`/`mov eax,imm32` of a handler that is `___CxxFrameHandler` or whose first 3 instructions jmp there.
- Tag values: `y` found; `-` not found; `?` not found but the body had decode errors (inlined always `?`).
